**Proposed fix.** In _plant_traits, force at least one planted admission per trait to come from a dev-bucket subject (or note in tests/fixtures/README that planted signal is not in buckets 0–4).

### FXT-9 — Adding a draw to build_plan reshuffles every table; the plan-level determinism rule is undocumented for EP-142/EP-148

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** forward-compat · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** fixture-generator (EP-11/12 synthetic fixture as the test substrate for every later brief)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/fixtures/spec.py:487`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/fixtures/spec.py:16`, `roadmap/EP-142-link-ed-ingestion.md:48`, `roadmap/EP-148-text-notes-staging.md:52`

**Evidence.** build_plan uses one sequential `default_rng(spec.seed)` across subjects/admissions; only tables use `table_rng`. spec.py docstring: 'adding a table (EP-12's icu generators) never perturbs the bytes of an existing one' — true for tables, false for any new plan draw (e.g. ED-only visits for mimiciv_ed.edstays, note counts). EP-12 avoided it implicitly ('build_plan ignores them'). AdmissionPlan has `ed/edregtime/edouttime` but no ED stay_id and no non-admitted ED visits.

**Impact.** EP-142's ED fixture (edstays needs discharged-from-ED visits, triage, vitalsign) and EP-148's notes fixture will need plan-level state; a naive addition inside the main loop silently changes all 31 hosp/icu files and every downstream count.

**Proposed fix.** Document the rule in spec.py and DESIGN §4: plan-level additions must draw from a dedicated child rng (`table_rng(spec, "plan.<stage>")`) after the main loop or be derived deterministically from existing plan fields; otherwise it is a minor GENERATOR_VERSION bump. Consider reserving `first_ed_stay_id` in FixtureSpec now.

### GOV-10 — Nothing pins the content of .claude/settings.json: a session (or merge) can weaken the deny list without any test, selfcheck or hook noticing

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** test · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Files:** `.claude/settings.json:1-3`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/guard.py:562-597`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep04.py:453-469`

**Evidence.** `_readme` says 'Do not weaken without updating GOVERNANCE.md and DECISIONS.md'; `selfcheck()` checks .gitignore/.gitattributes/.pre-commit-config/hook only; test_ep04 pins the pre-commit config in detail (lines 476-502) but never opens .claude/settings.json; TRACKED_PROBES only checks the file is not ignored. 67 rules today (EP-0 counted 67; unchanged since cd67743).

**Impact.** A future EP that trips on an over-broad rule (GOV-12) may 'fix' it by deleting the rule; the change is invisible to every green check and only a human diff review would catch it.

**Proposed fix.** XS: add `settings-deny-rules` selfcheck row (parse `.claude/settings.json`, assert a minimum set — the two data-location `Read//Grep//Glob` rules, `Read(**/*.csv)`, `Read(**/*.parquet)`, `Read(**/*.duckdb)`, `Bash(duckdb *)`, `Bash(*duckdb.exe*)`, `PowerShell(duckdb*)`, `PowerShell(Import-Csv *)` — and that no `allow` entry names mimicdata/source material) plus a test in test_ep04 asserting the rule count ≥ 67 and the minimum set; add a `git diff --cached --name-only` check in `mwh guard --staged` that prints a WARN line when `.claude/settings.json`, `.gitignore`, `.gitattributes` or `GOVERNANCE.md` is staged (a nudge, not a refusal).

### GOV-11 — Adding a new dataset directory under `source material/` (demo, ED 2.3, eICU, notes refresh) gets no Read/Grep deny coverage — the rules enumerate the three current folder names

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** forward-compat · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Files:** `.claude/settings.json:5-7,14-16`, `mimicwarehouse/GOVERNANCE.md:44`, `CLAUDE.md:22-25`

**Evidence.** Deny rules: `Read(./source material/mimic-iv-3.1/**)`, `…mimic-iv-ed-2.2/**`, `…mimic-iv-note-deidentified-free-text-clinical-notes-2.2/**` (and the same for Grep). `ls "source material"` shows exactly those three plus README.md today. The enumeration exists so `source material/README.md` stays readable. No brief, GOVERNANCE line, or selfcheck says 'new folder ⇒ new deny rules'.

**Impact.** The next credentialed download (a v3.2 refresh, ED update, or a P9 linkage dataset placed beside the others) is protected only by .gitignore/G2 and the Bash prefix rules; the Read/Grep tools would open its files.

**Proposed fix.** XS: (a) selfcheck row `source-material-deny-coverage`: for every top-level *directory name* under `source material/` (names only, via os.listdir — never opened) assert a `Read(./source material/<name>/**)` and `Grep(…)` rule exists; (b) one sentence in GOVERNANCE §2 and CLAUDE.md §6: 'a new folder under source material/ requires the matching deny rules before anything else'. Alternatively collapse to `Read(./source material/**)` and move `source material/README.md` content into the root README (owner decision).

### GOV-12 — Over-broad Bash deny rules block legitimate doc work: `grep`/`sed` on files merely mentioning 'source material' or 'mimicdata' (and on the allowed `source material/README.md`) are refused; CLAUDE.md does not warn

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** environment · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Files:** `.claude/settings.json:27-45`, `CLAUDE.md:22-27`, `mimicwarehouse/GOVERNANCE.md:8-9`

**Evidence.** Observed twice in this session: `grep -v "mimicdata\|C:\\\\"` over DESIGN.md → 'Permission to use Bash … has been denied' (`Bash(grep *mimicdata*)`); `grep -i "source material\|download…"` over DESIGN.md → denied (`Bash(grep *source material*)`). `Bash(sed *source material*)` likewise refuses `sed -n 1,50p "source material/README.md"` although CLAUDE.md §2 and GOVERNANCE line 8 direct sessions to read that README. `Bash(zcat *)`, `Bash(strings *)` are wholesale.

**Impact.** Not a leak — a productivity/confusion cost: sessions consolidating docs (EP-13 repos inventory, every re-plan) hit denials and may be tempted to 'fix' the rules (see GOV-10). The Read and Grep *tools* work for these files, so no rule change is needed.

**Proposed fix.** XS: keep the rules broad (safer) and add one line to CLAUDE.md §2: 'The Bash rules match the *string* — use the Read/Grep tools, not shell grep/sed, for docs that mention `source material` or `mimicdata`, and for `source material/README.md`'. Optionally narrow the shell rules to `*source material/mimic*` (all three data dirs start with `mimic-iv`) — but that reopens GOV-11.

### GOV-13 — Pre-commit hook works from GUI clients (uv is on the user PATH) but the Claude Bash tool inherits a stale PATH; CLAUDE.md does not carry the `export PATH` workaround — fail-closed but repeatedly rediscovered

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** environment · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Files:** `.pre-commit-config.yaml:8-9,14-16`, `CLAUDE.md:35-40`, `mimicwarehouse/README.md`

**Evidence.** `.git/hooks/pre-commit` exists (pre-commit-generated, `INSTALL_PYTHON=…\mimicwarehouse\.venv\Scripts\python.exe`), `core.hooksPath` unset, `hook-installed ok`. `reg query HKCU\Environment /v Path` contains `C:\Users\willi\AppData\Local\Microsoft\WinGet\Links` (uv.exe/uvx.exe there), so GitHub Desktop / VS Code Source Control resolve `uv run …` in the three `language: system` hooks. The Bash tool's PATH (`which uv` → not found) lacks that entry — inherited from the harness process, not a Windows misconfiguration; persistent memory records 'uv not on tool PATH (export before git commit too)' but CLAUDE.md §3 only says 'Run everything as uv run …'.

**Impact.** A fresh session without the memory note runs `git commit`, pre-commit reports 'Executable `uv` not found', the commit is refused (correct, fail-closed) and the session may reach for `--no-verify` (forbidden by CLAUDE.md §4.5) or an ad-hoc pre-commit env. If uv ever needs a `uv sync` at hook time (lock drift) it also needs network — offline commits fail closed.

**Proposed fix.** XS: add to CLAUDE.md §3: 'In Claude sessions run `export PATH="$LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/WinGet/Links:$PATH"` (Bash) once per shell before `uv`/`git commit`; hooks need `uv` on PATH.' Optionally record `Path` provenance in `mwh doctor` `uv` (info: 'found via WinGet Links / not on this shell's PATH').

### GOV-14 — CLAUDE.md §2 overstates deny coverage ('Repo .claude/settings.json denies these') and its allowed-*.md exception is contradicted by the shell rules

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** docs-drift · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Files:** `CLAUDE.md:22-27`, `.claude/settings.json:27-45,55-70`

**Evidence.** CLAUDE.md:22-25: 'Never print, read, head, cat, type, Get-Content, Select-String, grep, or open any file under source material/ (except *.md) or under the data root … and never run the duckdb executable directly. Repo .claude/settings.json denies these'. In fact: `Select-String *.csv` (outside the two roots) is not denied, `python -c`/`open()` is not denied (GOV-1), `Read` of `*.jsonl`/`.duckdb.new`/`.pkl` is not denied, and `cat "source material/README.md"` (an allowed *.md) IS denied.

**Impact.** A session that reads CLAUDE.md as the contract believes the settings enforce it and stops thinking; the honest statement is 'the deny rules refuse the common shell/Read forms; the rule is on you'.

**Proposed fix.** XS: reword to 'Repo .claude/settings.json denies the common forms (Read/Grep/Glob on the two data locations, cat/head/… on data paths, the duckdb executable); it is not exhaustive — python/uv-run/cp/nested shells are NOT blocked and are equally forbidden. Use the Read tool for `source material/README.md`.' Sync with GOVERNANCE §4(3) (GOV-8).

### GOV-15 — G4 does not scan report/template formats the roadmap will commit (.typ Typst, .j2/.jinja, .rst, .tex, .xml, .js/.css, .pdf) — the guard's 'text files' promise in GOVERNANCE §3 is narrower than P8 outputs

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** forward-compat · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Evidence.** TEXT_EXTENSIONS = `.py .md .yaml .yml .json .toml .sql .txt .csv .jsonl .html .svg .cff .ps1 .ini .cfg` (+ extensionless). EP-130 (Jinja → MD/HTML) and EP-131 (Typst PDF) will add `.j2`/`.jinja`/`.typ` templates and `.pdf` reports under docs/; `.pdf`, `.png`, `.xlsx` are never content-scanned. GOVERNANCE §3 says the hook refuses 'files containing ids in the real MIMIC bands' without qualification.

**Impact.** A rendered Typst source or a Jinja template with a hard-coded example id, or a committed PDF report whose text layer carries ids, passes G4; only `mwh disclose check` (EP-43, sidecar required for docs/) would catch it, and only if the session runs it.

**Proposed fix.** XS: extend TEXT_EXTENSIONS with `.typ .j2 .jinja .jinja2 .rst .tex .xml .js .css .ts .r .R .sh .bat .cmd .env .example .lock`? (`.lock` deliberately excluded per test — keep excluded) and, in the EP-131 brief, require `mwh disclose check` on every committed PDF; state the exact G4 file scope in GOVERNANCE §3.

### GOV-16 — Read deny list omits several data-shaped extensions that .gitignore/G1 do refuse (.duckdb.new/.tmp, .feather, .arrow, .pkl, .npy, .h5, .jsonl) and any upper-case variant (.CSV)

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** docs-drift · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Evidence.** Read denies only `*.csv *.csv.gz *.parquet *.duckdb *.duckdb.wal`; .gitignore lists `.duckdb.new .duckdb.tmp .wal .jsonl .feather .arrow .pkl .joblib .skops .pt .safetensors`; G1 adds `.npy .npz .h5`. `git check-ignore mimicwarehouse/x.CSV` → ignored (Windows core.ignorecase) but a `Read(**/*.CSV)` rule does not exist and Claude's glob matching is case-sensitive.

**Impact.** Minor: `Read` of a project-relative `.jsonl` ledger or `.feather` frame (e.g. a mis-set data root inside the workspace, or an EP-17 test that writes a `.feather` scratch frame into tests/) is not refused; binary formats render as noise but `.jsonl` is plain text rows.

**Proposed fix.** XS: add `Read(**/*.jsonl)`, `Read(**/*.duckdb.new)`, `Read(**/*.duckdb.tmp)`, `Read(**/*.feather)`, `Read(**/*.arrow)`, `Read(**/*.CSV)`, `Read(**/*.PARQUET)` and matching `Grep(**/*.jsonl)`; keep the three lists (settings deny, .gitignore, G1) generated from one table in guard.py and pin them with a test (GOV-10).

### GOV-7 — `!mimicwarehouse/tests/fixtures/**` re-includes every extension under fixtures (.pt, .duckdb, .pkl, .npy, .sqlite) — the .gitignore layer is fully open there; only G1 stands

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** bug · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Files:** `.gitignore:48-50`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/guard.py:96-99`

**Evidence.** `git check-ignore -v mimicwarehouse/tests/fixtures/model.pt` → `.gitignore:48:!mimicwarehouse/tests/fixtures/**` (not ignored); same for `x.duckdb`, `x.pkl`, `x.npy`, `x.sqlite`, `x.jsonl`. The two following lines (`!…/**/*.csv`, `!…/**/*.parquet`) are dead — the first negation already covers them. Comment at .gitignore:19-21 says 'Synthetic test fixtures are the only data-shaped files allowed, and only under mimicwarehouse/tests/fixtures/' but the intent (csv/parquet only) is not what the rule does.

**Impact.** A generated `.duckdb` cache or `.pt` model dropped under tests/fixtures by a future test helper (EP-17 catalog tests, P7 model tests) is not ignored and reaches the guard, which refuses it — fail-closed, but the .gitignore comment misleads and `git status` will show noise; if a session then adds it to FIXTURE_EXTENSIONS 'to make the commit work' the last layer is gone.

**Proposed fix.** XS: replace the three negations with `!mimicwarehouse/tests/fixtures/**/` (directories) plus explicit `!mimicwarehouse/tests/fixtures/**/*.{csv,csv.gz,parquet,jsonl,json,yaml,md}` lines matching FIXTURE_EXTENSIONS; add `mimicwarehouse/tests/fixtures/model.pt` to IGNORED_PROBES. Also note the existing probe `mimicwarehouse/tests/fixtures/hosp/patients.csv` (guard.py:151, EP-0) names a path that no longer exists (real tree is `fixtures/mimic-iv-3.1/hosp/patients.csv`) — harmless for check-ignore but update it.

### GOV-9 — No pre-push guard: `--no-verify`, `git rebase`/`cherry-pick`, or an un-installed hook on a fresh clone lets a violation reach the remote unnoticed until the EP-163 sweep

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Files:** `.pre-commit-config.yaml:12-19`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/guard.py:562-566`, `mimicwarehouse/GOVERNANCE.md:60-64`

**Evidence.** Config defines hooks for the default (pre-commit) stage only; `.git/hooks/` has `pre-commit` and no `pre-push`; `selfcheck` treats `hook-installed` as warn-level ('not installed - run: uv run --group dev pre-commit install') so `poe test`/`mwh verify` stay green on a clone with no hook. `scan_tracked` already exists as the primitive.

**Impact.** On a fresh clone (or GOV-2 collaborator machine) the first commits are guarded only by .gitignore; a rebase or cherry-pick that replays a bad commit runs no pre-commit hook; the remote is private until v1.0.0 so the blast radius is limited, but GOVERNANCE §3 says row-level data in history means filter-repo + remote rotation.

**Proposed fix.** S: add a `mwh-guard-push` local hook with `stages: [pre-push]`, `entry: uv run --project mimicwarehouse --group dev mwh guard --all-tracked` (~1 s on ~300 files) and document `pre-commit install --hook-type pre-commit --hook-type pre-push` in README/CLAUDE.md; extend selfcheck with `push-hook-installed` (warn). Optionally promote `hook-installed` to fail-level inside `mwh verify` for EPs that commit.

### INV-10 — Test coverage gaps: interrupted-run resume, `_atomic_write_text` retry path, resume blind spot for same-size rewrites within one clock tick, explicit 41 == len(contract.tables), and `finished` being written even on KeyboardInterrupt

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** test · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep10.py`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:503-516`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:858-887`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:149-151`

**Evidence.** No test simulates a build that stops after k files and resumes (only `test_build_records_rowcount_failure_and_continues`, which completes); no test triggers `PermissionError` in `os.replace`; scratch probe on this NTFS volume: an immediate same-size rewrite kept identical `(st_size, st_mtime_ns)` in 137/200 trials, so `matches_stat` cannot see it; `finally:` always sets `job["finished"] = result.finished` and appends a `runs` entry, so a Ctrl-C'd run looks 'finished' in `show` (only `files_done` betrays it); FILES_EXPECTED == len(contract.tables) is only implied via `len(result.processed) == FILES_EXPECTED`.

**Impact.** The two properties the completion note leans on most ('resumable', 'atomic rewrite after every file') are the least tested; a regression in the JSONL rewrite or in the skip logic would pass CI. The mtime blind spot matters only for synthetic/test files rewritten programmatically, but a future test that rewrites a fixture CSV with equal size may flake.

**Proposed fix.** Add: (a) monkeypatch `inventory_file` to raise RuntimeError on file k → assert k-1 JSONL lines, `last_file`, `finished` set with a `status`/`interrupted` marker (add `job["status"] ∈ {completed, interrupted, failed}`), then a resume processes exactly the rest; (b) monkeypatch `os.replace` to raise PermissionError twice → write succeeds and the sleep path is exercised; (c) a one-line assertion `FILES_EXPECTED == len(load_contract().tables)`; (d) document in the module docstring that the resume key is blind to same-size rewrites inside one timestamp tick and that `--force` is the remedy.

### INV-11 — Doc drift: DESIGN §11 has no raw-snapshot line, §3 tree lacks `lake\manifests\raw\`, §2 lacks the measured 2.0–2.4 GB/s raw read rate, README quick start lacks `mwh inventory`

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** docs-drift · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/DESIGN.md:310-323`, `mimicwarehouse/DESIGN.md:100-104`, `mimicwarehouse/DESIGN.md:28-40`, `mimicwarehouse/README.md:56-77`, `roadmap/EP-10-raw-inventory.md (completion note item 8)`

**Evidence.** DESIGN §11 ends with 'Snapshot id = hash of the layer manifest; every run cites the snapshot ids it read.' — no mention of the raw snapshot id, its canonical tuple or the `raw/` store (only the §15 module-map note has it). §3 tree: '└── manifests\  lake_manifests  <build_id>.jsonl → snapshot ids (EP-19)' — no `raw\`. §2 machine table + notes carry no read-throughput figure; the completion note item 8 says 'Worth carrying into the EP-17/18 loader estimates' and README Risk 1 repeats the numbers, but downstream briefs cite DESIGN §2. README quick start heading: 'EP-2 … EP-6: mwh --version, doctor, paths, guard, verify; the rest lands with EP-19'.

**Impact.** EP-16/17/19 sessions read §11/§3/§2 first and will not find the raw-snapshot definition or the throughput fact; the manifests directory ends up with two conventions (EP-10 subdir vs EP-17 flat files) with no design sentence saying so.

**Proposed fix.** Dated notes: §11 (raw snapshot id = sha256(json(sorted (rel_path, bytes, sha256, rows))), None until complete, store path, `raw_snapshot_id()` accessor, relation to EP-17 lines); §3 tree add `│   │   └── raw\   (EP-10) <dataset-dir>.jsonl + raw_snapshot.json`; §2 note 'raw read throughput measured 2.0–2.4 GB/s with Defender real-time on (EP-10)'; README quick-start line for `mwh inventory build|show|reconcile`.

### INV-14 — No manifest schema version: FileRecord is `extra="forbid"` and JSONL lines are parsed with `model_validate_json`, so adding a required field or removing one breaks `show`/`build` on the existing store

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** forward-compat · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:100-103`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:531-541`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:623-640`

**Evidence.** `model_config = ConfigDict(frozen=True, extra="forbid")`; `rec = FileRecord.model_validate_json(line)` with no try/except; `raw_snapshot.json` carries `canonical` but no `manifest_version`.

**Impact.** The first schema evolution (e.g. INV-2's per-record contract_hash, or an EP-17 `source_sha256` alias) makes `load_raw_manifest` raise on the 41 existing lines; the remedy is a `--force` rebuild (≈90 s) but `show` — the only window sessions have on the job — crashes until then.

**Proposed fix.** Add `MANIFEST_VERSION = 1` to the snapshot and give every new FileRecord field a default; on parse failure raise a clear `ManifestVersionError('run mwh inventory build --force')` instead of a pydantic traceback.

### INV-15 — `open_connection` is a de-facto DuckDB connection factory living in inventory.py (and leaks a connection per bare `inventory_file` call); EP-17 asks for exactly one implementation

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:353-360`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:452-454`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/fixtures/catalog.py:78`, `roadmap/EP-17-stage-loader-core.md:30-35`

**Evidence.** `open_connection(settings)` → `duckdb.connect(database=":memory:", config=dict(settings.duckdb_settings("build")))`; `inventory_file`: `con = connection if connection is not None else open_connection()` — never closed; `fixtures/catalog.py:78` opens its own `duckdb.connect(":memory:", config=config)`. EP-17: 'If EP-3 already ships a connection factory in `config.py`, wrap it — one implementation only.'

**Impact.** EP-17 will add a third factory or import one from a module whose docstring says it is the raw-inventory module; DuckDB settings drift (memory_limit/temp dir) between inventory, fixtures and the loader becomes possible.

**Proposed fix.** At EP-17, move `open_connection(settings, profile="build", database=":memory:")` into `config.py` (or a tiny `db.py`) and re-export from inventory; use a `contextlib.closing` in `inventory_file` when it opens its own connection.

### INV-16 — `show` hides row-count failures: a new run resets `errors` and only header mismatches are listed, so a record with `rowcount_method == "failed"` is visible only in `reconcile` as `pending`

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:783`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:1277-1288`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:1180-1200`

**Evidence.** `job = {..., "errors": [], ...}` at every build start; `_job_lines` prints `errors {len(errors)}` from the snapshot; `show` lists `bad = [r ... if not r.header_matches_contract]` only. A resumed run does retry failed counts (`prev.rows is None` → todo), but if it fails again the manifest line says `failed` while `show` prints `errors 1` only until the next run.

**Impact.** EP-16/EP-20 verifying through `show` (the only permitted window) can read `41/41 files in manifest … errors 0` while one file has `rows=null` and the snapshot id was computed with a null tuple.

**Proposed fix.** In `show`, list records with `rowcount_method in {failed, skipped}` (count + names) next to the header-mismatch list, and add `files_with_rows` to the summary line; test it with the existing `broken` monkeypatch.

### INV-5 — FILES_EXPECTED=41 and DATASET_DIRS are hard-coded next to a contract that already knows the datasets: adding a dataset/table silently drops it from `plan_files` and KeyErrors in `reconcile`; the snapshot id would be issued at 41/42

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** forward-compat · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** M · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:72-80`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:564-573`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:605`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:656-660`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:745-747`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:933-937`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/contract.py:49`

**Evidence.** `FILES_EXPECTED = 41`; `compute_snapshot_id(records, *, files_expected=FILES_EXPECTED)` returns an id as soon as `len(tuples) >= files_expected`; `build_inventory` computes `expected_per_dataset` from the contract but `flush()` calls `write_snapshot` with the default `files_expected`. `plan_files`: `wanted = ... else set(DATASET_DIRS)` then `if t.dataset not in wanted: continue` (silent). `reconcile`: `expected_by_dataset = {d: expected_counts(d, contract) for d in DATASET_DIRS}` then `manifest.records.get(f"{dataset_dir(t.dataset)}/…")` → `KeyError('unknown dataset …')` for a contract table whose dataset is not in DATASET_DIRS. contract.py has its own `DATASETS` tuple; the PhysioNet directory names live only in inventory.py.

**Impact.** The moment a P9 EP (EP-142/145) or a re-download adds a table or dataset to the contract, `mwh inventory reconcile` crashes and `build` issues a 'complete' raw_snapshot_id over an incomplete manifest (41 of 42), which downstream manifests then cite.

**Proposed fix.** Derive `files_expected = len(plan_files(contract, …))` (or `sum(expected_per_dataset.values())`) inside `build_inventory`/`show`/`render_docs`, keeping the constant only as a test assertion `FILES_EXPECTED == len(load_contract().tables)`; add an import-time/test assertion `set(DATASET_DIRS) == set(contract.DATASETS)`; consider moving the directory name into the contract YAML (`SchemaInfo` already carries `csv_dir`; a per-dataset `source_dir` would make the contract the single source of truth).

### INV-6 — `header_status == "order"` must count as NOT ok for the loader, because `read_csv(columns={…})` assigns names positionally — EP-17's brief only asks for a missing/extra-*names* check

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** forward-compat · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:134-141`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:343-350`, `roadmap/EP-17-stage-loader-core.md:36-44`, `mimicwarehouse/docs/resources/raw-inventory.md (Files section blurb)`

**Evidence.** Inventory: `matches = got == expected` (order-sensitive), `header_status` returns `"order"` when names match but order differs and `header_matches_contract` is False — correct. EP-17 brief: 'read_csv(\'<path>\', header=true, columns={<name: type from contract>}, …)' and 'validate the file header (read only the first line, names only) against the contract … a mismatch raises SchemaMismatchError listing missing/extra column *names*'. DuckDB's `columns=` option names columns in file order and skips the header line, so a same-names/different-order file loads with swapped columns if the check is set-based.

**Impact.** A future re-download whose header order differs from create.sql (or a demo file after the column map) would pass a names-only check and be staged with silently transposed columns; the raw inventory would say `order` but nobody consumes that state.

**Proposed fix.** State in the EP-17 brief (item 2) and DESIGN §5 that the pre-load header check is order-sensitive (list equality after the column map) and that the loader may treat inventory `header_status != "ok"` as a hard stop for the credentialed source root; keep inventory's semantics as-is and add one sentence to the docs page blurb ('order = same names, different order — not loadable positionally').

### INV-7 — docs page `Generated:` uses the wall clock, so every `mwh inventory reconcile` dirties a tracked file even when nothing changed

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** process · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:1002`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:1293-1316`, `roadmap/EP-10-raw-inventory.md (Delta 1: 'regenerates the page byte-for-byte except the Generated timestamp')`

**Evidence.** `a(f"- **Generated:** {_now()}")`; `reconcile_command` defaults to `--docs` and writes `docs/resources/raw-inventory.md` under the workspace (`write_docs` → `docs_path()`), i.e. into git-tracked space.

**Impact.** Every EP that runs `reconcile` as a check (EP-16, EP-20, EP-28) leaves `git status` dirty with a one-line diff; sessions either commit noise or must remember to revert; combined with INV-1 the diff is not timestamp-only anyway.

**Proposed fix.** Make the page a pure function of the manifest: print `snap.get("updated_at")`/`finished` as 'Manifest updated' instead of `_now()` (or omit the line); optionally default `--no-docs` for the CLI check path and require `--docs` to regenerate. Adjust `test_docs_page_is_a_manifest_only` accordingly.

### INV-8 — Stale manifest records are never pruned: a renamed csv_path/dataset dir leaves the old line in the JSONL forever, enters the snapshot id, and inflates files_done past files_expected

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:739`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:836-837`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:569-571`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:1216`

**Evidence.** `manifest = load_raw_manifest(settings, root)` loads every existing line; `write_dataset_manifest(root, p.dataset, manifest.by_dataset(p.dataset))` rewrites all loaded records of the dataset, planned or not; `compute_snapshot_id` only checks `len(tuples) < files_expected` (never `>` or membership); `show` computes `pending = planned − records` but not `records − planned`.

**Impact.** After any contract path change the id is computed over 41 current + N orphan tuples, `show` prints `42/41 files in manifest`, and the docs page lists a file that no longer exists; `--force` does not clear it either (records not in `todo` are kept).

**Proposed fix.** In `build_inventory`, drop records whose `rel_path` is not in the planned set (log `orphan: <rel>`; keep them only when `--dataset`/`--max-bytes` filtering excludes their dataset), and make `compute_snapshot_id` require the tuple set to equal the expected rel_path set rather than a count. Test: rename one csv_path in a copied contract → orphan pruned, id recomputed.

### INV-9 — CLI: hidden `--force` help says 'same as --no-resume' but `--no-resume` is not an accepted flag, and `--force` is registered twice

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** bug · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:1132-1143`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep10.py:660-663`

**Evidence.** `typer.Option("--resume/--force", …)` plus a second parameter `typer.Option("--force", help="Recompute every file (same as --no-resume).", hidden=True)`. Probe via CliRunner against an absent source root: `['inventory','build','--force','--quiet']` → exit 2 'source root … does not exist' (parsed OK); `['inventory','build','--no-resume','--quiet']` → click usage error (No such option). The test comment '--force is accepted' precedes an invocation without `--force`.

**Impact.** An operator following the hidden help types `--no-resume` and gets a usage error; the duplicate registration relies on click's last-wins behaviour and will confuse `--help` generation if typer changes.

**Proposed fix.** Keep one declaration: `--resume/--no-resume` (default resume) plus a separate visible `--force` flag, or `--resume/--force` alone; delete the hidden duplicate; add a CliRunner test that `--force` and the chosen negative flag both parse.

### SCH-11 — The 13 `source: docs` foreign keys are not uniform with the upstream FK style

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** schema-contract (EP-9) as the spine of later briefs

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/keys.yaml:10`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/keys.yaml:139`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/keys.yaml:151`

**Evidence.** Upstream constraint.sql gives every ICU event table a `subject_id -> patients` FK in addition to `stay_id -> icustays`; the docs FKs give ED child tables (diagnosis, medrecon, pyxis, triage, vitalsign) only `stay_id -> edstays` and note `*_detail` tables only `note_id -> discharge/radiology`, no `subject_id -> patients`. The header explicitly parks 'provider ids, emar -> pharmacy/poe, transfers -> admissions ... to EP-28's integrity tests', so hosp documented links are also absent while ED/Note documented links are present.

**Impact.** If EP-28/EP-44 iterate `contract.foreign_keys` (the natural design), subject_id integrity of five ED tables and two note tables is tested only transitively and the hosp documented links not at all; the two data sources of FK truth (keys.yaml vs EP-28 test code) will diverge.

**Proposed fix.** State the policy in keys.yaml (either 'docs FKs list every documented link, drift-checked = false' and add ~12 rows: ED children -> patients, note details -> patients, emar -> pharmacy/poe, emar_detail -> pharmacy, transfers -> admissions, *_provider_id -> provider, caregiver_id -> caregiver; or 'docs FKs only tie the module roots into hosp' and let EP-28 own the rest). Recommend the former so EP-28 has one list.

### SCH-12 — `mwh schema list` is unreadable at the 80-column width autonomous sessions see (names folded mid-word)

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** environment · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** schema-contract (EP-9) as the spine of later briefs

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/cli.py:93`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/cli.py:106`

**Evidence.** Observed via the Bash tool (non-tty, rich defaults to width 80): rows render as `| mimiciv_icu | 10   | stay_id,ite | charttime   | subject_id, | large | yes  |` / `| .datetimeev |      | mid,chartti |             | charttime   |       |      |` / `| ents        |      | me          |             |             |       |      |` - `rt.add_column(col, overflow="fold")` folds `mimiciv_icu.datetimeevents` across three lines. `mwh schema show` output is fine.

**Impact.** Every later session that runs `mwh schema list` in the tool (EP-16 verification, EP-17/18/19 planning) gets a table it cannot parse by eye; `--json` works but is verbose.

**Proposed fix.** Give the `table` column `no_wrap=True, min_width=28` (or set `Console(width=120)` when `not console.is_terminal`), and drop the `unit_of`/`comment` fold to `overflow="ellipsis"` only where the console is a tty. Also add `UnitsSpec.unit_for(table, column) -> str | None` (fixed_units/implied_units are currently reachable only by iterating) for EP-29's `unit_hint`, and delete the unused `iter_tables` / `Contract.has_table`.

### SCH-4 — REAL -> FLOAT (4-byte) for four columns and INTEGER sbp/dbp in vitalsign contradict the 'one numeric type per family' rationale used for resprate

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** owner-decision · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** schema-contract (EP-9) as the spine of later briefs

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_hosp.yaml:299`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_hosp.yaml:366`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_ed.yaml:97`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_ed.yaml:116`, `mimicwarehouse/DECISIONS.md:129`

**Evidence.** D-17 addendum: 'the single NUMERIC(10, 4) column (ed.vitalsign.resprate) is DOUBLE so one table does not mix Decimal and float vitals'. Yet the only four FLOAT (float32) columns in the whole contract are `pharmacy.basal_rate / doses_per_24_hrs / duration` and `prescriptions.doses_per_24_hrs` ('upstream REAL'), and `mimiciv_ed.vitalsign.sbp/dbp` are `INTEGER` while `mimiciv_ed.triage.sbp/dbp` are `DOUBLE` - the ed.yaml header says 'Units are fixed by the ED data dictionary ... sbp/dbp mmHg' as one family.

**Impact.** Polars/pandas frames from EP-49/EP-102 get float32 columns only for these four (0.3 -> 0.30000001192 after promotion), and any UNION of triage and vitalsign vitals (EP-34 events spine, EP-142 ED linkage) needs explicit casts. Cosmetic today, a recurring cast/rounding nuisance later.

**Proposed fix.** Either keep (faithful to upstream, zero effort) or set the four REAL columns to DOUBLE with `upstream_type: REAL` and vitalsign.sbp/dbp to DOUBLE with `upstream_type: INTEGER` (six one-line YAML edits, drift oracle still guards them; SCH-2 regen applies). Recommend DOUBLE for consistency with the resprate decision.

**Owner question.** Keep REAL -> FLOAT and vitalsign INTEGER sbp/dbp as upstream-faithful, or widen the six columns to DOUBLE (recorded via upstream_type) so measurements are one type warehouse-wide? Recommendation: widen, in the same commit as SCH-2's structural hash so the fixture manifest is regenerated once.

### SCH-5 — sort_keys conventions are not uniform across modules and do not always follow time_column

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** schema-contract (EP-9) as the spine of later briefs

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_ed.yaml:58`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_ed.yaml:106`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_icu.yaml:28`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_hosp.yaml:222`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_hosp.yaml:372`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_hosp.yaml:127`, `roadmap/EP-18-stage-loader-buckets.md:27`

**Evidence.** ED event tables sort `[subject_id, stay_id, charttime]` (medrecon, pyxis, vitalsign) whereas ICU event tables sort `[subject_id, charttime]` (chartevents, outputevents...) and hosp `emar` `[subject_id, charttime]` without hadm_id; `procedures_icd` has `time_column: chartdate` but sorts `[subject_id, hadm_id, seq_num]` while `hcpcsevents` (also chartdate) sorts `[subject_id, chartdate]`; `microbiologyevents` has `time_column: charttime` but sorts `[subject_id, chartdate, charttime]`; `emar_detail` sorts on VARCHAR `parent_field_ordinal` ('1.10' < '1.2' lexicographically). EP-18: '`sort_by` defaults to the contract's `sort_keys` minus the leading `subject_id`'.

**Impact.** EP-18's Parquet row-group statistics prune differently for ED vs ICU events; EP-34/EP-49 timeline queries by (subject_id, time) get worse locality on ED tables; nobody can tell which convention a new table should follow. Not a correctness bug.

**Proposed fix.** Write the rule into the hosp.yaml header and DESIGN section 5 ('event tables: [subject_id, <NOT NULL date/time>, <tie-breaker>]; detail tables: [subject_id, <parent id>, <ordinal>]; dims: natural key'), then either drop stay_id from the ED events or add stay_id/hadm_id to the ICU/hosp events, and align procedures_icd with hcpcsevents. Decide before EP-18 writes the lake (a re-sort later means a full rebuild).

### SCH-6 — load_class is a hand-picked list with no stated threshold

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** docs-drift · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** schema-contract (EP-9) as the spine of later briefs

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/transcribe.py:310`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_hosp.yaml:9`, `mimicwarehouse/docs/resources/raw-inventory.md`, `roadmap/EP-18-stage-loader-buckets.md:29`

**Evidence.** `LARGE_TABLES: frozenset({"chartevents", "labevents", ... "radiology"})` (13 names, 'Brief item 2'); the YAML header only says 'metadata (time_column, sort_keys, load_class)'. Cross-checked against docs/resources/raw-inventory.md: the 13 are exactly the files >= 909 MB (microbiologyevents 909,240,343 bytes is the smallest 'large'; outputevents 462,494,098 bytes is the largest 'small'), matching EP-18's 'Small path (CSV <= ~1 GB)'.

**Impact.** The classification is consistent today but undocumented; when EP-26/EP-27 tune the two-pass loader or a future MIMIC release changes sizes, nobody knows whether to reclassify (e.g. outputevents at 0.46 GB, poe_detail 0.42 GB).

**Proposed fix.** Record the rule in the hosp.yaml header and DESIGN section 5: 'load_class = large iff raw CSV >= ~0.9 GB (EP-10 inventory) - the two-pass path of EP-18'; optionally a test that derives the set from the inventory page bytes.

### SCH-7 — ColumnMap/TableMap are generic models with version-specific field names, and the only map is the identity

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** forward-compat · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** schema-contract (EP-9) as the spine of later briefs

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/contract.py:302`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/contract.py:332`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/column_maps/demo_2_2_to_3_1.yaml:272`

**Evidence.** `class TableMap(_Frozen): added_in_3_1 ... dropped_in_3_1 ...`; `ColumnMap.tables_absent_in_2_2`; the shipped file ends `tables: {}` / `tables_absent_in_2_2: []` with 37 identity tables. EP-17 item 2 and EP-22 will code against these names.

**Impact.** A second map (MIMIC-IV 3.2, ED 2.3, a P9 external source through the same shim) would carry fields literally named `added_in_3_1`; renaming after EP-17/EP-22 use them costs two modules plus tests. The file itself is not dead weight: it is the D-27 evidence record and the header-validation contract for EP-22.

**Proposed fix.** Rename to `added_in_target` / `dropped_in_target` / `tables_absent_in_source` (model + YAML + test_ep09 + one line in EP-17's brief) now, while no consumer exists; keep the file and the `derivation` prose. Regenerate the fixture manifest (SCH-2).

### SCH-8 — Dataset label / directory / module knowledge is duplicated in four places and the contract has no slot for demo sources or validate_demo.sql

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** schema-contract (EP-9) as the spine of later briefs

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/contract.py:49`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/contract.py:439`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/transcribe.py:337`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:76`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/fixtures/write.py:49`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/concepts/vendor/VENDOR.json`

**Evidence.** contract.py `DATASETS = ("mimic-iv-3.1", "mimic-iv-ed-2.2", "mimic-iv-note-2.2")` + `SchemaInfo(name, dataset, csv_dir, ...)`; transcribe.py `_CSV_DIR = {...}` / `_DATASET = {...}` re-state the YAML headers; inventory.py `DATASET_DIRS = {"mimic-iv-note-2.2": "mimic-iv-note-deidentified-free-text-clinical-notes-2.2", ...}` (PhysioNet dir names); fixtures/write.py `DATASET_DIR = "mimic-iv-3.1"`, `MODULE_SCHEMAS = {HOSP_DIR: "mimiciv_hosp", ICU_DIR: "mimiciv_icu"}`. VENDOR.json `excluded` lists `mimic-iv-ed/buildmimic/postgres/validate_demo.sql` ('not in the EP-8 allow-list') although build_mimic.sh selects `validate_demo.sql` when 100 patients are found; the contract's `expected_rows_source` is single-valued (full only).

**Impact.** EP-22 (demo raw root `ext\demo\mimic-iv-demo-2.2\{hosp,icu}`, `.csv.gz` names) and EP-142 (ED) will add a fifth and sixth mapping; the demo build has no upstream row-count expectation to reconcile against, unlike full/ED.

**Proposed fix.** Add a `datasets:` block to the contract (label -> physionet_dir, version, license, demo_dir, csv_suffixes, expected_rows_source per tier) and make inventory.py / fixtures / transcribe / EP-22 read it; vendor `mimic-iv/buildmimic/postgres/validate_demo.sql` and the ED one at EP-16 (VENDOR.json allow-list change, EP-8 lens).

### SCH-9 — DDL parser drops any column whose name starts with a constraint keyword (no word boundary)

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** bug · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** schema-contract (EP-9) as the spine of later briefs

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/transcribe.py:157`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/transcribe.py:255`

**Evidence.** `_CONSTRAINT_WORDS = ("PRIMARY", "CONSTRAINT", "FOREIGN", "UNIQUE", "CHECK")` and `if any(upper.startswith(w) for w in _CONSTRAINT_WORDS): return None` operate on the whole `name TYPE` text without a `\b`, so a column line `checkin_time TIMESTAMP` or `unique_key INTEGER` is silently treated as a table constraint. No current MIMIC column starts with these words (grep of the three create.sql files: none), so `mwh schema check` is unaffected today.

**Impact.** If a future upstream pin adds such a column, `mwh schema transcribe` omits it and `mwh schema check` cannot report the missing_column drift, defeating the oracle's purpose for that column.

**Proposed fix.** Use `re.match(r"^(PRIMARY|CONSTRAINT|FOREIGN|UNIQUE|CHECK)\b", upper)`; add one synthetic-DDL test case in test_ep09::test_parse_create_sql_synthetic.

### VT-10 — EP marker range 0..199 leaves 35 slots for re-plan insertions; overflow surfaces as a --strict-markers collection error far from its cause

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** forward-compat · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/tests/conftest.py:47`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/conftest.py:118`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep01.py:265`

**Evidence.** conftest.py:47 `EP_MARKER_RANGE = range(0, 200)  # ep_0 … ep_199`; roadmap max EP is 164 and EP-164 was itself an insertion at the P0 re-plan (README.md:71); 11 re-plans remain. test_ep01.py:265 only pins `"ep_199:" in markers`.

**Impact.** A re-plan that assigns EP-200+ makes `pytestmark = pytest.mark.ep_200` a collection error ('ep_200 not found in markers configuration option'), and `mwh verify EP-200` exits 2 with the misleading pytestmark hint.

**Proposed fix.** Widen to `range(0, 1000)` (cost: 1000 addinivalue_line calls, negligible) or register markers from `roadmap/EP-*.md` numbers plus a margin; note the ceiling in tests/README.

Note test_ep06.py:723-726 pins two README needles (`uv run --group dev mwh verify EP-<n>`, `uv run poe roadmap-check`) — keep them.

### VT-12 — _console_safe (cp1252 guard) is a private helper in verify.py while DESIGN and Risk 13 name it as the house rule for every CLI table; doctor/inventory print `·`/`—` without it

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:673`, `mimicwarehouse/DESIGN.md:66`, `roadmap/README.md:395`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/doctor.py:816`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/cli.py:55`

**Evidence.** verify.py:673 `def _console_safe(text: str) -> str:` (underscore, not exported in __all__); DESIGN.md:66 "or pass through `verify._console_safe` (roadmap Risk 13)"; Risk 13: "Every CLI table or log line added by P1+ ... must either stay ASCII or pass through the same helper"; doctor.py:816-819 summary line uses `·` and `—` with no helper (rich raises UnicodeEncodeError on strict cp1252 streams — rich/console.py:2118 re-raises).

**Impact.** Later EPs (inventory tables, build logs, EP-19 runner) either import a private symbol from an unrelated module or forget the rule and crash under a cp1252 host (the EP-3 `mwh paths` incident).

**Proposed fix.** Move it once, process-wide: in the `mwh` app callback call `sys.stdout.reconfigure(errors='replace')` (and stderr) before creating the rich Console, or expose `console_safe()` publicly from `mimicwarehouse.cli`/a tiny `console.py`; keep verify's calls as thin wrappers; add one test that renders doctor/verify output through a cp1252 `io.TextIOWrapper` with errors='strict'. Update DESIGN §15 note + Risk 13 wording.

### VT-13 — Tests build real Settings from the environment (get_settings().duckdb_settings('app')) — temp_directory points under the real data root and an unsafe MWH_DATA_ROOT would error unrelated tests

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** test · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep09.py:316`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep11.py:154`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/config.py:637`

**Evidence.** test_ep09.py:316 / test_ep11.py:154 `config = dict(get_settings().duckdb_settings("app"))` → config.py:637 `"temp_directory": str(self.layout["tmp_duckdb"])` (under `data_root`); no monkeypatch/tmp root in either place. No real data is read (in-memory DuckDB, synthetic frames), so no license issue.

**Impact.** On a machine where MWH_DATA_ROOT is unset/unsafe (OneDrive, missing volume) test_ep09/test_ep11 error in Settings validation instead of testing DDL/fixtures; a memory spill would create files under the real root during `poe test`.

**Proposed fix.** Add a session fixture `duckdb_test_config` in conftest built from `Settings(data_root=tmp_path_factory.mktemp('root'))` (or `duckdb_settings('app')` with temp_directory overridden) and use it in both places; grep-guard: no `get_settings()` in tests without a fixture/monkeypatch except the deliberate tier probes.

### VT-3 — Hard-coded fixture/table counts (31 / 22 / 9) in test_ep11, test_ep12, conftest and tests/README force edits to old test files whenever the fixture tree grows

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** test · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep11.py:758`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep11.py:760`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep11.py:771`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:542`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:658`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:696`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:904`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:908`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:913`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/conftest.py:199`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/README.md:41`

**Evidence.** `git diff f1fccf1 ac4f51e -- tests/ep/test_ep11.py`: `-    assert "wrote 22 files" in result.output` → `+    assert "wrote 31 files"`, `len(payload["files"]) == 22` → `== 31`; EP-12 completion note: "`tests/ep/test_ep11.py` needed three small edits". test_ep12.py:658 `assert len(list(DATASET_DIR.rglob("*.csv"))) == 31`; :542 `assert len(keys) == 31 and manifest["modules"] == ["hosp", "icu"]`. Future briefs add fixture trees: EP-137 `tests/fixtures/ext/`, EP-148 `tests/fixtures/notes/`, EP-19 reads the tree.

**Impact.** The next EP that adds a fixture module (or a manifest entry) must edit test_ep11 and test_ep12 again; the counts duplicate what test_ep09 already asserts about the contract.

**Proposed fix.** Derive expectations from the contract in both modules: `N_HOSP = len(contract.by_schema('mimiciv_hosp'))`, `N_ICU = len(contract.by_schema('mimiciv_icu'))`, `N_31 = len(contract.by_dataset('mimic-iv-3.1'))`, and compare `manifest['modules']` / file sets against those; the 'wrote N files' CLI assertion becomes f-string based. Keep one explicit pin (`== 31`) in test_ep09 where the contract is the subject. Reword conftest/tests README to 'the mimic-iv-3.1 contract tables' instead of '31'.

### VT-4 — roadmap_check spawns two git processes per ☑ hash and is run 4× per test session — grows ~10× by roadmap end

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** performance · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:384`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:396`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:400`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:527`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep06.py:376`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep06.py:648`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep06.py:696`

**Evidence.** commit_exists() and commit_subject() each call `_run_git` per sha; measured `uv run mwh verify --roadmap --json` = 1.02 s wall with 15 hashes; durations: test_mwh_verify_roadmap_cli 1.39 s, test_roadmap_check_main_and_script 1.07 s, test_real_roadmap_has_zero_errors 0.67 s. With ~165 done rows (≥165 hashes) that is ≥330 git spawns ≈ 6-7 s per call, ×4 in test_ep06.

**Impact.** `poe test` and every session-end `mwh verify --roadmap` slow down linearly with progress (~25 s added to the suite by P11); not blocking, but avoidable.

**Proposed fix.** Resolve all hashes in one call: `git log --all --format=%H%x09%s` (or `git cat-file --batch-check` with `input=`) into a dict, then prefix-match; keep `_run_git` as the single seam so test_ep06's fake_git only needs to answer one call shape. Also let test_ep06 call the real-roadmap check once (module-scoped fixture) and reuse the Report.

### VT-5 — verify() exit-code contract: EXIT_USAGE = 2 collides with pytest's INTERRUPTED/collection-error code 2, and the 'no tests collected' hint ignores tier deselection

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** design · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:57`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:1`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:653`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:659`

**Evidence.** verify.py:57 `#: Exit codes of :func:`verify` beyond pytest's own (0 pass, 1 failures, 3/4 internal/usage).` — pytest 2 (Interrupted, incl. 'error during collection') is not mentioned yet `EXIT_USAGE = 2`. verify.py:653-659: `if proc.returncode == PYTEST_NO_TESTS_COLLECTED: echo(... is 'pytestmark = pytest.mark.ep_{n}' set in {module.name}?)` — a module whose tests are all tier('dev') under the default tier also exits 5.

**Impact.** A syntax error in test_epNN.py (pytest 2) is indistinguishable from an mwh usage error (2) for scripts/re-plan sessions reading exit codes; a dev-only module gets a misleading 'is pytestmark set?' hint.

**Proposed fix.** Either remap usage to a code pytest never uses (e.g. 64) and document the full mapping in the module docstring/README, or at minimum document that 2 is shared; extend the exit-5 hint with 'or every test is above the selected tier (--tier)'.

### VT-6 — mwh verify collects the entire tests tree for one marker; the end-to-end EP-2 verify test is the slowest test in the suite (3.2 s) and both grow with every module

**Severity:** low · **Kind:** performance · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:604`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/verify.py:659`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep06.py:311`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep06.py:325`

**Evidence.** pytest_argv(): `[sys.executable, '-m', 'pytest', '-m', f'ep_{ep}', '-p', 'no:cacheprovider', *pytest_args]` — no path, so all 12 modules (polars, duckdb, pydantic imports) are collected per verify; `--durations`: `3.22s call tests/ep/test_ep06.py::test_mwh_verify_ep2_end_to_end`. grep shows exactly one `pytestmark = pytest.mark.ep_<n>` per module and no cross-module ep markers.

**Impact.** Each `mwh verify EP-n` pays full collection (~0.7 s now, tens of seconds at 165 modules with heavier imports); the e2e test grows the same way.

**Proposed fix.** When `ep_test_module(n)` exists append its path to argv (`pytest -m ep_n tests/ep/test_epNN.py`); keep `-m` so the semantics stay 'the marker set'. Document that a brief's tests live in exactly one module (already the convention). Update test_pytest_argv_shape accordingly.

### VT-7 — Duplicated test scaffolding across nine modules (five fresh-interpreter 'import stays light' tests, cli_env/isolated_root fixtures, WORKSPACE/REPO constants, _write/_sha256, COLUMNS=200, CliRunner) — no shared tests/helpers

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep02.py:142`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep06.py:730`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep09.py:854`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep11.py:786`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:927`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep04.py:98`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep06.py:67`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:755`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep10.py:85`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep11.py:751`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:898`

**Evidence.** `WORKSPACE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]` appears in test_ep01/03/04/05/06/08/09/11/12; `runner = CliRunner()` in 8 modules; `cli_env` fixture identical in test_ep04.py:98 and test_ep06.py:67; `monkeypatch.setenv("COLUMNS", "200")  # rich: no cell folding` in ep10/11/12; five subprocess tests all run `python -c "import sys, mimicwarehouse.cli; ...heavy modules..."` (0.31-0.36 s each ≈ 1.7 s of the 31 s suite).

**Impact.** Every new EP copies the boilerplate again (drift in what 'heavy' means: ep02 lists 4 modules, ep12 lists 5); fixes to the isolation pattern must be made in N places.

**Proposed fix.** Add `tests/helpers.py` (WORKSPACE/REPO/ROADMAP, `write_text`, `sha256`, `run_python(code)`) and conftest fixtures `clean_mwh_env`, `isolated_data_root`, `wide_console`, `cli_runner`; collapse the five import-budget tests into one parametrised `tests/test_import_budget.py` (or keep it under ep_2 as the CLI owner) with a single HEAVY tuple that later EPs extend. Do it as one consolidation commit, not per EP.

### ARCH-16 — Catalogs embed absolute lake paths and assert DuckDB version equality on open: not relocatable, every pin bump forces a rebuild of all tier catalogs + runs.duckdb

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** forward-compat · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-166 · **Lens:** data architecture parity/uniformity (lake / catalog / tier / DAG design vs EP-3/9/10/11/12 code)

**Files:** `roadmap/EP-21-stage-catalog-builder.md:24-33,41-47`, `mimicwarehouse/DESIGN.md:191-210`, `roadmap/EP-52-backup-state.md`, `roadmap/EP-158-bootstrap-init-cloner.md`

**Evidence.** EP-21 item 1: views are `read_parquet('<abs glob>' ...)`; item 2: `open_catalog` "asserts the catalog's recorded DuckDB version equals the running one"; DESIGN section 6: pin one DuckDB version, storage format changes 1.4 -> 1.5.

**Impact.** Moving the data root (EP-52 restore, EP-158 init on another machine, a `--data-root` override) leaves views pointing at the old absolute paths; a duckdb pin bump makes every catalog refuse to open until `mwh build --select catalog` runs per tier - both fine as long as they are documented as 'catalogs are disposable, rebuild them'.

**Proposed fix.** State in DESIGN section 6 that catalogs are derived, disposable and rebuilt (seconds) after a data-root move or pin bump; EP-52 backs up nothing under `warehouse/`; consider `read_parquet` paths relative to a `SET file_search_path`/lake root recorded in `catalog_info` when EP-158 lands.

### DOC-16 — CLAUDE.md '(except *.md)' allowance for source material/ holds only for the Read tool; Bash/PowerShell deny rules refuse `cat`/`grep` of anything containing 'source material'

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** environment · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-166 · **Lens:** documentation drift and internal contradictions across all prose (README/CLAUDE/NOTICE/DESIGN/DECISIONS/GOVERNANCE/tests+fixtures+brand READMEs/raw-inventory/source-material README/roadmap README+final-roadmap/completion notes)

**Files:** `CLAUDE.md:18`, `.claude/settings.json`, `mimicwarehouse/GOVERNANCE.md:80`

**Evidence.** CLAUDE.md:18-21: "**Never** ... `cat` ... any file under `source material/` (except `*.md`) ...". `.claude/settings.json` (67 deny rules, 0 allow) contains `Bash(cat *source material*)`, `Bash(grep *source material*)`, `PowerShell(Get-Content *source material*)` etc. — this session's `cat -n "source material/README.md"` was denied; the Read tool returned it.

**Impact.** A session following CLAUDE.md literally hits a denial on the README it is told it may read and may waste turns or, worse, try a workaround (CLAUDE.md forbids working around denials). Not a safety gap — the opposite.

**Proposed fix.** CLAUDE.md:18-21 add: "(read `source material/README.md` with the Read tool; every shell command whose text contains 'source material' or 'mimicdata' is denied outright, including on `.md`)".

### FC-30 — D-42 / Malwarebytes allow list is path-based (workspace .venv, uv managed python) — fine for venv recreation, but EP-19's spawn choice and any workspace move must respect it

**Evidence.** D-38 addendum table: "Malwarebytes 5.1 Premium allow list — seven paths: `C:\Program Files\Git`, `%APPDATA%\uv\python`, workspace `.venv`, `C:\mimicdata`, `source material\`, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Microsoft\WinGet\Packages\astral-sh.uv_*`, `%USERPROFILE%\.cache\pre-commit`"; D-42: bulk writers "run under the allow-listed managed `python.exe` from the allow-listed `.venv`". EP-16 item 6 puts the seven-path list on the P2 readiness checklist.

**Impact.** Recreating `.venv` in place keeps the allow list valid; a spawn via `pwsh`/`cmd` wrappers or a relocated workspace does not. EP-19's `Start-Process pwsh` fallback and detached `uv run` are unlisted binaries (harmless unless they write bulk data themselves).

**Proposed fix.** State in EP-19: the detached child is `<workspace>\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m mimicwarehouse.cli …` (allow-listed path); EP-16 readiness note records that the list is path-based and must be redone if the checkout moves.

### FXT-16 — Byte-count and item-count drift in notes/YAML (5,370,673 vs 5,370,674; '~44 items' vs 45)

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** docs-drift · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** fixture-generator (EP-11/12 synthetic fixture as the test substrate for every later brief)

**Evidence.** manifest.json `total_bytes` = 5370674 (sum of 31 entries); EP-12 completion note and DESIGN §4 note both say '5,370,673 bytes = 5.12 MiB'. d_labitems.yaml version_note says '~44 items' while 45 items are listed (DESIGN §15 says 45).

**Impact.** Cosmetic; a future audit comparing the manifest with the notes wastes a minute.

**Proposed fix.** Correct the two notes to 5,370,674 (or 'about 5.37 MB') and the YAML note to 45; fold into the next docs pass, no regen needed (version_note is not read by the generator — confirm before editing, else it changes d_labitems.csv? it does not: version_note is not a column).

### GOV-17 — NOTICE and pyproject declare MIT with a repository-root LICENSE that does not exist until EP-163

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** docs-drift · **When:** owner-decision · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-165 · **Lens:** governance enforcement layers (deny rules, mwh guard, .gitignore/.gitattributes, hooks, GOVERNANCE text vs reality)

**Files:** `NOTICE:26`, `mimicwarehouse/pyproject.toml:10`, `mimicwarehouse/GOVERNANCE.md:127`

**Evidence.** NOTICE line 26: 'Our own code is MIT-licensed (repository-root LICENSE, EP-163).' `ls` of the repo root shows no LICENSE file; pyproject.toml:10 `license = "MIT"`; GOVERNANCE §10: 'Code license: MIT (LICENSE at the repo root from EP-163…)'. Consistent as a plan, but any `uv build`/sdist made before EP-163 ships an MIT metadata claim without the text, and the vendored mimic-code MIT notice (NOTICE) sits beside a package that itself carries no license file.

**Impact.** Cosmetic until the remote goes public; a collaborator (GOV-2) or a wheel built for the app (P4) sees inconsistent metadata.

**Proposed fix.** XS: either add the 21-line MIT LICENSE now (owner's name/year) and reword NOTICE/GOVERNANCE 'from EP-163' → 'since EP-<n>', or leave as is and note in EP-163's In-scope that the file is created there.

**Owner question.** Add the MIT LICENSE file now (XS, no downside) or keep it for EP-163 as planned? Recommendation: add now; the plan text only needs a date tweak.

### INV-12 — Snapshot id includes `rows`, so the id depends on the counter (DuckDB version/options) and a `--no-rowcount` pass yields a second id for identical bytes — accepted in delta 5 but the DuckDB-bump angle is unrecorded

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** design · **When:** owner-decision · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:560-573`, `roadmap/EP-10-raw-inventory.md (Delta 5)`, `mimicwarehouse/DECISIONS.md:196-207`

**Evidence.** `canonical_tuples` = sorted `(rel_path, bytes, sha256, rows)`; `rows` is a function of DuckDB 1.5.5 + `CSV_READ_OPTIONS`; sha256 already determines content. Delta 5 discusses only the `--no-rowcount` case.

**Impact.** A future DuckDB pin bump that changes a count (parser fix) or a `--force` rebuild after `--no-rowcount` changes the raw_snapshot_id although no raw byte changed, so lake manifests citing the old id look stale for no data reason.

**Proposed fix.** Owner decision, low urgency: (a) keep as is and record in DESIGN §11 that the id is content+count and that a counter change legitimately re-issues it; (b) redefine the id over `(rel_path, bytes, sha256)` and keep rows as an attribute (one-line change; today's id would change once). Recommendation: (a) now, since 8209301d… is already cited in DECISIONS/README and the counts reconcile.

**Owner question.** Should raw_snapshot_id stay content+rows (current, cited in D-26) or become content-only (rel_path, bytes, sha256)? Recommendation: keep, and document the consequence in DESIGN §11.

### INV-13 — PermissionError retry is adequate only because the data root is excluded from both AV engines; `write_docs` into the (scanned) repo tree is non-atomic

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** environment · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:503-516`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:1078-1084`, `roadmap/EP-10-raw-inventory.md (Delta 7)`

**Evidence.** 20 retries with `time.sleep(0.05 * (attempt + 1))` ≈ 10.5 s total on `os.replace` only (`tmp.write_text` is not retried); the manifest store lives under `<data_root>/lake/manifests/raw` which D-38/D-42 record as excluded in Defender and Malwarebytes, so scanner locks are not expected there; the docs page is written with a plain `write_text` into `mimicwarehouse/docs/resources/`, which Defender does scan.

**Impact.** None observed; if exclusions lapse (new machine, EP-158 bootstrap) a scanner holding the fresh `.tmp` could exhaust the retry and abort a build mid-file (resumable, so recoverable). A crash mid `write_docs` leaves a truncated tracked page.

**Proposed fix.** Optional: reuse `_atomic_write_text` in `write_docs`; add a one-line note in the module docstring that the retry budget assumes the data-root exclusions; make EP-158 (bootstrap) list the exclusions as a checked precondition.

### INV-17 — `lake/manifests/raw/` is module-owned, not a `Settings.layout` key — acceptable, but the convention (EP-10 subdir vs EP-17/19 flat `<build_id>.jsonl`, `status.json`, `snapshots.json`) is undocumented

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** design · **When:** owner-decision · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-167 · **Lens:** raw inventory / provenance module (EP-10)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:81-82`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/inventory.py:487-496`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/config.py:78-90`, `roadmap/EP-17-stage-loader-core.md:64-66`, `roadmap/EP-19-stage-dag-runner.md:55`

**Evidence.** `RAW_DIRNAME = "raw"  #: not a Settings.layout key; created here`; `LAYOUT_KEYS` has 15 entries ending at `lake_manifests`; EP-17 writes `lake/manifests/<build_id>.jsonl` and `lake/manifests/status.json`, EP-19 `lake/manifests/snapshots.json` — all flat.

**Impact.** Low: `mwh paths` never lists or creates the raw store, and `mwh doctor` cannot report it; nothing breaks, but EP-17 may add `lake_manifests_status` etc. ad hoc and test_ep03's '15 keys' assertion becomes a moving target.

**Proposed fix.** Owner decision: (a) keep module-owned subpaths and add one DESIGN §3/§5 sentence: 'layout keys are directories mwh paths creates; files/subdirs under them are owned by their module (`inventory.manifest_dir()`, EP-17 `manifest.py`)'; (b) add `lake_manifests_raw` as a 16th key (touches config, `mwh paths`, test_ep03, README). Recommendation: (a).

**Owner question.** Add `lake_manifests_raw` (16th layout key) or keep module-owned subpaths documented once in DESIGN? Recommendation: keep module-owned, document the rule.

### SCH-10 — Nullable time columns and nullable PK members have no stated policy in the contract

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** design · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-169 · **Lens:** schema-contract (EP-9) as the spine of later briefs

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_hosp.yaml:200`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/mimiciv_hosp.yaml:60`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/keys.yaml:22`, `mimicwarehouse/src/mimicwarehouse/schema/tables/keys.yaml:37`

**Evidence.** Computed from load_contract(): time_column is nullable in 13 tables (labevents.charttime, pharmacy.starttime, prescriptions.starttime, transfers.intime, icustays.intime, hcpcsevents.chartdate, microbiologyevents.charttime, medrecon/pyxis/vitalsign.charttime ...); PK members nullable in the contract: diagnoses_icd.icd_code/icd_version, procedures_icd.icd_code/icd_version, services.curr_service, procedureevents.orderid (create.sql omits NOT NULL; Postgres' ADD PRIMARY KEY would enforce it) and prescriptions.drug (deliberately relaxed). uniqueness_hints also include nullable columns (emar_detail.parent_field_ordinal, medrecon.charttime/name/gsn/etc_rn).

**Impact.** EP-18 sorts NULL times last (fine); EP-34's events spine and EP-49 timeline need a fallback rule (storetime? chartdate?) per table; EP-28/EP-44 uniqueness tests must decide NULL semantics; EP-29's `nullable` in meta.columns will say PK columns may be NULL, which is true in DuckDB (no constraint) but not upstream's effective schema.

**Proposed fix.** Add a one-paragraph policy to DESIGN section 7 (and hosp.yaml header): 'time_column may be NULL; the spine uses <fallback> per table (EP-34 decides)'; either mark PK members NOT NULL via `nullable: false, upstream_nullable: true` (documented as PK-implied) or state that nullability follows create.sql verbatim and PK NULL handling belongs to EP-28. Purely documentary; no code change needed now.

### VT-14 — Doc-string pins inside tests: rewriting README/DESIGN/tests README during this consolidation will trip them unless the needles are preserved

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** process · **When:** now-should · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep04.py:509`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep05.py:325`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep06.py:723`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep12.py:848`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep03.py:585`

**Evidence.** test_ep04.py:509-510 README must contain `pre-commit install` and `mwh-guard: allow`; test_ep06.py:724-726 README must contain `uv run --group dev mwh verify EP-<n>` and `uv run poe roadmap-check`; test_ep12.py:848-854 tests/README must contain `PYTEST_TIER`, `--tier`, `fixture < dev < full`, `demo`, `default_tier`, DESIGN must contain `EP-12` and `PYTEST_TIER`, .env.example must NOT contain `PYTEST_TIER`; test_ep05.py:325-329 root README `<picture>` banner markup; test_ep03.py:585-589 .env.example / .gitignore parity.

**Impact.** An otherwise correct docs consolidation goes red in ep_3/4/5/6/12; conversely, these are the only mechanised doc checks, so they are worth keeping.

**Proposed fix.** No code change required; before committing doc edits run `uv run poe test -m "ep_3 or ep_4 or ep_5 or ep_6 or ep_12"`. Optionally centralise the needles in one `tests/test_docs_pins.py` so future EPs know where the pins live.

### VT-15 — Lint/type gates are minimal for a test-heavy repo: pyright basic, no flake8-pytest-style rules; poe check does not include guard/roadmap-check

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** process · **When:** owner-decision · **Effort:** S · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/pyproject.toml:87`, `mimicwarehouse/pyproject.toml:90`, `mimicwarehouse/pyproject.toml:110`

**Evidence.** `[tool.ruff.lint] select = ["E", "F", "I", "B", "UP", "SIM", "RUF"]` (no `PT`, `ARG`, `PTH`); `typeCheckingMode = "basic"` (untyped test helpers such as `_count(con, sql...)`, `def con():` pass silently); `check = ["lint", "typecheck", "test"]` — the roadmap check only runs indirectly via test_ep06 and `guard` only via the pre-commit hook.

**Impact.** Not a defect; a policy gap the owner may want closed before 150 more test modules are written in the same style.

**Proposed fix.** Trial `ruff check --select PT` and `pyright --level standard` once, record counts, and decide; consider `check = ["lint", "typecheck", "guard", "test"]` so a session's `poe check` also runs the guard on staged files.

**Owner question.** Tighten now (add ruff PT rules, pyright standard, guard in `poe check`) or leave until the P1 re-plan? Recommendation: measure both once (10 min), adopt PT + guard-in-check if zero/low noise, defer pyright standard.

### VT-8 — pytest-xdist is installed but unused; a trial run shows -n works on the current suite

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** environment · **When:** owner-decision · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/pyproject.toml:42`, `mimicwarehouse/pyproject.toml:98`, `mimicwarehouse/README.md:47`

**Evidence.** `uv run pytest -q -n 4 tests/ep/test_ep06.py tests/ep/test_ep12.py tests/ep/test_ep03.py tests/ep/test_ep10.py` → `139 passed in 7.18s` (serial ep06+ep12 alone = 13.5 s). Tests use tmp_path/tmp_path_factory data roots, `-p no:cacheprovider` in nested runs, per-process settings cache; session `fixture_catalog` (~0.6 s) is rebuilt per worker. README.md:47 dev-group list omits pytest-xdist.

**Impact.** None today (31 s is fine); as the suite grows toward 165 modules the serial time will matter, and the dependency is unexplained in docs.

**Proposed fix.** Add `test-fast = "pytest -n logical"` to poe (keep `test` serial so `-ra` skip output stays readable) and mention pytest-xdist in README's dev-group list; or drop the dependency if the owner prefers a leaner dev group.

**Owner question.** Keep pytest-xdist and expose `poe test-fast` (-n logical), or remove it until the suite is slow? Recommendation: keep + add the task now (one line), since the trial shows it is safe.

### VT-9 — hypothesis is imported at every pytest start (conftest profile registration) but no property test exists yet (FIX-3 parked)

**Severity:** info · **Kind:** test · **When:** next-replan · **Effort:** XS · **Retro episode:** EP-168 · **Lens:** verification tooling & test infrastructure (verify.py, roadmap_check, conftest tiers, tests/ep/*, pyproject test/lint config)

**Files:** `mimicwarehouse/tests/conftest.py:33`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/conftest.py:210`, `mimicwarehouse/tests/ep/test_ep01.py:261`, `roadmap/final-roadmap.md:185`

**Evidence.** conftest.py:33 `from hypothesis import settings as hypothesis_settings`; only consumer is test_ep01::test_conftest_registers_markers_and_hypothesis_profile. Measured import cost 0.23 s per interpreter (`python -c 'import hypothesis'`), paid by every `mwh verify`, every xdist worker and the copied conftest in pytester sessions. final-roadmap.md:185 parks the property fixtures to v2 FIX-3.

**Impact.** Small fixed overhead and a doc claim ('pytest + hypothesis', DESIGN §20) that is aspirational; not harmful.

**Proposed fix.** Either accept (document in tests/README that hypothesis is registered for FIX-3/later EPs) or lazy-load: register profiles inside `pytest_configure` guarded by `importlib.util.find_spec('hypothesis')`. Not dead weight per se — several P3+ briefs may use it.
